# CharacterQuilt Brand Profiles

> Machine-readable brand identity profiles for thousands of companies — colors, fonts, logos, and visual systems extracted from each company's live website. Designed for agents, designers, and AI tools.

## How to use

- Each company has a public HTML page at `/branding/<slug>` and a JSON profile at `/branding/<slug>.json`.
- The JSON profile includes the full Firecrawl branding extraction: colors, typography, components, personality, and source images.
